`soft_top_indices(c, M, K)`:

```
If all equal, uniform.

def lp_step(f, M, maxiter, timeout_s):

   A=np.column_stack? Build:

   # optional scale: A has small ~1/n, b=1. Feasible h ~n. OK.
   res=linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=np.ones(2*n-1), bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'output_flag':0})
   h=res.x; sumh=h.sum(); if <=0 return None
   p_new=h/sumh
   # blend f with p_new: try t in [0.2,0.5,1.0]

      cand=(1-t)*f + t*p_new; cand[np.minusc]<=0; cand/=sum
      M_new=score_internal(cand)
      if M_new < M: update f
   return f, M_new

Pseudo:
```
import numpy as np, time, math
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog

def _safe_array(x): ...

def _normalize_positive(p, floor=0.0):
   p=np.asarray(p,float)
   p=np.where(np.isfinite(p),p,0)
   p=np.clip(p,0,None)
   s=p.sum()
   if s<=0: p=np.ones_like(p)/len(p)
   else: p/=s
   return p

def _score_internal(p):
   if p.size==0: return np.inf
   p=_normalize_positive(p)
   c=np.convolve(p,p)
   M=float(c.max())
   return 2*p.size*M

def _softmax(x):
   x=np.asarray(x,float)
   x=x-np.max(x)
   p=np.exp(x)
   s=p.sum()
   if s<=0 or not finite: uniform
   return p/s
```

Power:
```
def _power_seq(n, alpha, mirror=0.0, shift=1.0):
   i=np.arange(n,float)
   w=(i+shift)**(-alpha)
   if mirror:
      w += mirror*((n-1-i)+shift)**(-mirror_alpha?) # use alpha
   w += 1e-15
   return w/w.sum()
```
Maybe `mirror` small parameter: w = (i+1)^-alpha + gamma*((n-i)^-alpha). For gamma=0.

Random initial:
```
def _random_seq(n, rng, shape='lognormal'):
   x=rng.normal(0,0.3,n)
   p=np.exp(x); return p/p.sum()
```

def _lp_refine(p0, max_iters=12, deadline=None):

```
def high_indices(c, M, maxK):
   tol = max(1e-12, 0.05*M)
   idx=np.flatnonzero(c >= M - tol)
   if idx.size==0: idx=np.argmax(c)[None]
   if idx.size>maxK:
       idx=idx[np.linspace(0, idx.size-1, maxK).astype(int)]
   return idx
```

def upscale(p, m):

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    deadline=t0+max(0.0,float(budget_s)-15.0)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_seq=None; best_score=np.inf
    def consider(p): nonlocal best_seq,best_score; ... try evaluator? internal score; if finite and <best_score: best=p
    # parent
    try: p=... consider(p)
    except: consider(np.ones(256))
    # candidate generation for lengths list based on parent size
    plen = parent.size if valid else 5582
    lengths=[]
    for n in [64,128,256,512,1024,2048,4096,plen]: if >1 and n<=8192? add unique sorted
    for n in lengths:
       for alpha in [0.5,0.48,0.45,0.4,0.35,0.3]:
          if time<deadline: consider(_power_seq(n,alpha))
          if alpha==0.5: consider(_power_seq(n,0.5,mirror=0.1))
       # random few for n<=256
    # smooth refine on best if size<=1024 else maybe 512?
    if best_seq is not None and len(best_seq)<=1024 and time<deadline-100:
        p,r = _smooth_refine(best_seq, deadline=deadline, max_seconds=120)
        consider(p)
    # lp refine on length 256 and best if <=256
    if time<deadline-60:
        p256=_power_seq(256,0.5)
        p,r=_lp_refine(p256, deadline=deadline, max_time=30)
        consider(p)
        if len(best_seq)<=256 and time<deadline-30:
           p,r=_lp_refine(best_seq, ...)
    # active refine best
    if time<deadline-10:
        p,r=_active_refine(best_seq, deadline=deadline, rng=rng, max_time=...)
        consider(p)
    # If best_score still near 2, try active from power length 2048?
    # Final
    final=best_seq if not None else uniform
    # scale? keep sum 1
    final=np.clip(final,0,1000); final=final/sum? If sum1; after operations maybe sum1.
    val=list(map(float, final))
    # if evaluate inf, fallback parent? We can compute evaluate.
    sc=evaluate_sequence(val)
    if not finite: val=[1.0]
    return val
```

Implement `upscale`:
```
def _upscale(p, m):
   p=_normalize_positive(p)
   n=p.size
   if m==n: return p
   c=np.cumsum(p)
   x_old=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   x_new=np.linspace(0,1,m+1)
   c_new=np.interp(x_new, x_old, c)
   q=np.diff(c_new, prepend=0.0)
   # first/last? c_new[-1]=1
   q=np.clip(q,0,None)
   s=q.sum(); if s<=0: uniform
   return q/s
```

```
pos = idx
wpos = ...
g=np.zeros(n)
for k, wt in zip(pos,wpos):
   L=max(0,k-(n-1)); R=min(n-1,k)
   if L<=R:
      # indices i=L..R, p index k-i from k-L down to k-R
      g[L:R+1] += wt * p[k-L:k-R+1][::-1]
```

```python
import time
import math
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog


def _normalize_positive(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        p = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p = np.ones_like(p, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(p.sum())
    p = p / s
    return p


def _softmax(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    m = float(np.max(x))
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return None
    x = x - m
    with np.errstate(under="ignore", over="ignore"):
        p = np.exp(x)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return None
    p = p / s
    return p


def _internal_score(p):
    p = _normalize_positive(p)
    if p.size <= 1:
        return 2.0
    c = np.convolve(p, p)
    M = float(c.max())
    return float(2.0 * p.size * M)


def _power_sequence(n, alpha=0.5, mirror=0.0):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    w = (1.0 + i) ** (-float(alpha))
    if mirror != 0.0:
        w = w + float(mirror) * (1.0 + (n - 1 - i)) ** (-float(alpha))
    w = np.clip(w, 0.0, None)
    w = w + 1e-15
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        w = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        w = w / s
    return w


def _random_sequence(n, rng, strength=0.35):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    x = rng.normal(0.0, float(strength), n)
    p = _softmax(x)
    if p is None:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    return p


def _upscale(p, m):
    p = _normalize_positive(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    m = int(max(1, m))
    if n == m:
        return p
    c = np.cumsum(p)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m + 1)
    c_new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, c)
    q = np.diff(c_new)
    q = np.clip(q, 0.0, None)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / m
    return q / s


def _active_weights(c, M, n):
    tol = max(1e-14, 0.05 * M)
    idx = np.flatnonzero(c >= M - tol)
    if idx.size == 0:
        idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)
    max_k = min(256, max(16, n // 10))
    if idx.size > max_k:
        sel = np.linspace(0, idx.size - 1, max_k).astype(int)
        idx = idx[sel]
    raw = float(c[idx]) - (M - tol)
    raw = np.maximum(np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64), 0.0)
    raw = raw + 1e-12
    w = raw / float(raw.sum())
    return idx, w


def _random_perturb(p, rng, strength=0.12):
    p = _normalize_positive(p)
    with np.errstate(under="ignore", over="ignore"):
        u = np.exp(np.clip(rng.normal(0.0, float(strength), p.size), -6.0, 6.0))
    q = p * u
    s = float(q.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return p
    return q / s


def _active_refine(p0, deadline, rng, max_seconds=None):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    p = _normalize_positive(p0)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return p
    c = np.convolve(p, p)
    M = float(c.max())
    best = p.copy()
    bestM = M
    step = 0.10
    stale = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if max_seconds is not None and (time.monotonic() - t_start) > max_seconds:
            break

        idx, w = _active_weights(c, M, n)
        g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for k, wt in zip(idx, w):
            L = max(0, int(k) - (n - 1))
            R = min(n - 1, int(k))
            if L <= R:
                g[L:R + 1] += wt * p[int(k) - R:int(k) - L + 1][::-1]
        acw = float(np.dot(w, c[idx]))
        d = p * (g - acw)
        d -= float(np.dot(p, d))
        maxd = float(np.max(np.abs(d))) if d.size else 0.0

        accepted = False
        if math.isfinite(maxd) and maxd > 1e-14:
            base = step / maxd
            for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 2.0, 0.125):
                alpha = base * mult
                if not math.isfinite(alpha) or alpha <= 0.0:
                    continue
                u = np.clip(-alpha * d, -30.0, 30.0)
                with np.errstate(under="ignore", over="ignore"):
                    p_try = p * np.exp(u)
                s = float(p_try.sum())
                if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
                    continue
                p_try = p_try / s
                c_try = np.convolve(p_try, p_try)
                M_try = float(c_try.max())
                if math.isfinite(M_try) and M_try < M - 1e-13:
                    p, c, M = p_try, c_try, M_try
                    step = min(0.5, step * 1.12)
                    stale = 0
                    if M < bestM:
                        best, bestM = p.copy(), M
                    accepted = True
                    break
            if not accepted:
                step = max(0.004, step * 0.75)
                stale += 1
        else:
            stale += 1

        it += 1
        if stale >= 50:
            p_try = _random_perturb(p, rng, strength=0.25)
            c_try = np.convolve(p_try, p_try)
            M_try = float(c_try.max())
            if math.isfinite(M_try) and M_try < M - 1e-13:
                p, c, M = p_try, c_try, M_try
                if M < bestM:
                    best, bestM = p.copy(), M
            stale = 0
            step = 0.10

    return best


def _smooth_refine(p0, deadline, max_seconds=120.0, max_n=512):
    p0 = _normalize_positive(p0)
    n = int(p0.size)
    if n <= 1 or n > int(max_n):
        return p0

    p0 = p0 + 1e-12
    p0 = p0 / float(p0.sum())
    xlog = np.log(np.maximum(p0, 1e-300))
    x0 = xlog - xlog[0]
    x0[0] = 0.0
    x0 = np.clip(x0, -18.0, 18.0)

    L = 2 * n - 1
    best_p = p0.copy()
    best_M = _internal_score(p0)
    t_start = time.monotonic()

    def objective_grad(xvec):
        x = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
        x[0] = 0.0
        if n > 1:
            x[1:] = np.asarray(xvec, dtype=np.float64)
        x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
        x = np.clip(x, -18.0, 18.0)
        p = _softmax(x)
        if p is None:
            p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        c = np.convolve(p, p)
        maxc = float(c.max())
        beta = objective.beta
        z = beta * (c - maxc)
        with np.errstate(under="ignore", over="ignore"):
            e = np.exp(z)
        Z = float(e.sum())
        if Z <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(Z):
            w = np.zeros_like(c)
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
            F = maxc
        else:
            w = e / Z
            F = (maxc + math.log(Z)) / beta
        conv_w = np.convolve(p, w[::-1])
        g = conv_w[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
        grad_p = 2.0 * g
        S = float(np.dot(p, grad_p))
        grad_x = p * (grad_p - S)
        jac = grad_x[1:]
        return F, jac

    objective.beta = 1
    bounds = [(-18.0, 18.0)] * max(0, n - 1)

    beta_candidates = [
        int(max(1, 8 * n)),
        int(max(1, 20 * n)),
        int(max(1, 40 * n)),
        int(max(1, 80 * n)),
    ]
    xcur = x0.copy()

    for bi, beta in enumerate(beta_candidates):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0:
            break
        if max_seconds is not None and (time.monotonic() - t_start) > max_seconds * (bi + 1) / len(beta_candidates):
            break
        objective.beta = beta
        try:
            res = minimize(
                objective_grad,
                xcur,
                jac=True,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={
                    "maxiter": 120 if n <= 256 else 70,
                    "maxls": 40,
                    "ftol": 1e-11,
                    "gtol": 1e-7,
                    "disp": False,
                },
            )
        except Exception:
            break
        if hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
            xcur = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            xcur = np.where(np.isfinite(xcur), xcur, 0.0)
            xcur = np.clip(xcur, -18.0, 18.0)
            xfull = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
            xfull[0] = 0.0
            if n > 1:
                xfull[1:] = xcur
            p = _softmax(xfull)
            if p is not None:
                M = _internal_score(p)
                if math.isfinite(M) and M < best_M:
                    best_p, best_M = p, M

    return best_p


def _lp_refine(p0, deadline, max_seconds=40.0, max_n=512, max_iters=None):
    p = _normalize_positive(p0)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 1 or n > int(max_n):
        return p

    if max_iters is None:
        max_iters = 30 if n <= 128 else (16 if n <= 256 else 8)

    best = p.copy()
    best_M = _internal_score(p)
    t_start = time.monotonic()

    for _ in range(int(max_iters)):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0:
            break
        if (time.monotonic() - t_start) > max_seconds:
            break

        p_lp = p + 1e-13
        p_lp = p_lp / float(p_lp.sum())

        A = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
        scale = float(n)
        for i, val in enumerate(p_lp):
            if val > 0.0:
                A[i:i + n, i] = val * scale

        b = np.full(2 * n - 1, scale, dtype=np.float64)
        c_obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        hi = float(10.0 * n + 10.0)
        try:
            res = linprog(
                c_obj,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=b,
                bounds=[(0.0, hi)] * n,
                method="highs",
                options={"output_flag": 0},
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        if res is None or getattr(res, "success", False) is False:
            x = getattr(res, "x", None)
            if x is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64))):
                break
            h = np.clip(np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64), 0.0, None)
        else:
            h = clip = None
            h = np.clip(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64), 0.0, None)

        s = float(h.sum())
        if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
            break
        h = h / s

        cur_M = _internal_score(p)
        improved_current = False
        for t in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0):
            q = (1.0 - t) * p + t * h
            q = _normalize_positive(q)
            q_M = _internal_score(q)
            if math.isfinite(q_M) and q_M < best_M:
                best, best_M = q, q_M
            if improved_current is False and math.isfinite(q_M) and q_M < cur_M:
                p, cur_M = q, q_M
                improved_current = True

        if not improved_current:
            break

    return best


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 20.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    def consider(p):
        nonlocal best_seq, best_score
        try:
            q = _normalize_positive(p)
            if q.size == 0:
                return
            sc = _internal_score(q)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-15:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = q.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Parent initialization, if present.
    parent = None
    try:
        parent = _normalize_positive(height_sequence_1)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        consider(parent)
    else:
        parent = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64) / 256.0
        consider(parent)

    plen = int(parent.size) if parent.size > 0 else 5582
    plen = int(np.clip(plen, 64, 8192))

    # Cheap broad initial batch across useful lengths and exponents.
    lengths = []
    for n in (64, 128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048, 4096, plen):
        n = int(n)
        if n > 1 and n not in lengths:
            lengths.append(n)
    lengths.sort()

    for n in lengths:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 60.0:
            break
        for alpha in (0.50, 0.48, 0.45, 0.40):
            consider(_power_sequence(n, alpha))
        consider(_power_sequence(n, 0.50, mirror=0.10))
        if n <= 256:
            for _ in range(4):
                consider(_random_sequence(n, rng, strength=0.30))

    # If a short solution is clearly better, try upscaling it to the parent length.
    if best_seq is not None and best_seq.size < plen and time.monotonic() < deadline - 80.0:
        consider(_upscale(best_seq, plen))
        consider(_upscale(_power_sequence(512, 0.50), plen))

    # Smooth low-dimensional-ish refinement on medium sizes.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 120.0:
        for n_smooth in (256, 512):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 30.0:
                break
            p_seed = _power_sequence(n_smooth, 0.50)
            p_out = _smooth_refine(p_seed, deadline, max_seconds=70.0, max_n=512)
            consider(p_out)
            if p_out.size == n_smooth and time.monotonic() < deadline - 20.0:
                consider(_upscale(p_out, plen))

    # LP active-set style iteration on small-to-medium sizes.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 80.0:
        for n_lp in (128, 256, 512):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 20.0:
                break
            p_seed = _power_sequence(n_lp, 0.50)
            p_out = _lp_refine(p_seed, deadline, max_seconds=25.0, max_n=512)
            consider(p_out)
            if p_out.size == n_lp and time.monotonic() < deadline - 15.0:
                consider(_upscale(p_out, plen))

    # Active-set projected refinement on the best candidate found so far.
    if best_seq is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 10.0:
        p_out = _active_refine(best_seq, deadline, rng, max_seconds=None)
        consider(p_out)

    # One more active refinement from a large power-law seed if needed.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 10.0:
        large_seed = _power_sequence(min(max(1024, plen), plen), 0.50)
        p_out = _active_refine(large_seed, deadline, rng, max_seconds=None)
        consider(p_out)

    if best_seq is None:
        final = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        final = _normalize_positive(best_seq)
        final = np.clip(final, 0.0, 1000.0)

    val = [float(x) for x in final]
    try:
        sc = float(evaluate_sequence(val))
    except Exception:
        sc = float("inf")

    fallback = [1.0]
    try:
        sc_fb = float(evaluate_sequence(fallback))
    except Exception:
        sc_fb = 2.0

    if (not math.isfinite(sc)) or (math.isfinite(sc_fb) and sc_fb < sc):
        val = fallback

    # Last guard: make sure the object really is a nonempty finite list.
    if not isinstance(val, list) or len(val) == 0:
        val = [1.0]
    cleaned = []
    for x in val:
        if isinstance(x, bool) or not isinstance(x, (int, float)):
            cleaned.append(1.0)
        else:
            fx = float(x)
            if math.isnan(fx) or math.isinf(fx):
                cleaned.append(1.0)
            else:
                cleaned.append(max(0.0, min(1000.0, fx)))
    if not cleaned:
        cleaned = [1.0]

    return cleaned
```
